P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-L — Controlled Activity Intake activity_events Column Mapping Audit
Created: 25 May 2026, Europe/Warsaw

Status
COLUMN MAPPING AUDIT CREATED / DOC ONLY / NO PERSIST HELPER / NO RUNTIME ROUTE / NO DATABASE WRITE / NO SQL EXECUTION / NO AI CALL / NO ACTIVITY CREATED

Baseline
- Previous block: P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-K
- Previous commit: 02ec450
- Previous message: docs: add C8-I-D4-L-L-O-K persist helper design gate

Files intentionally not created in O-L
- lib/activity/controlledIntake/persist.ts
- src/app/api/activity/controlled-intake/route.ts

Main rule
Activity Event remains the source of truth.
Controlled intake persist helper, when later approved, may insert only one raw activity_events row.
It must not persist semantic candidates, Value Objects, state facts, deltas, or snapshots.

Audit purpose
O-K blocked direct persist.ts implementation until exact activity_events column mapping is confirmed.
O-L performs a local static audit of schema files, migrations, generated DB types, docs, routes, and existing activity code references.

Search roots
docs, lib, src, supabase, scripts

activity_events total reference count
2263

Candidate groups with evidence
title_or_name, raw_input_or_description, source, status, duration, started_at, ended_at, occurred_at, timezone, metadata_json, trusted_owner_context, idempotency_storage

Candidate groups with weak/no evidence


Minimum groups required for a safe future persist helper
title_or_name, raw_input_or_description, status, metadata_json, trusted_owner_context

Missing minimum groups
(none)

gateDecision:
PASS_LOCAL_EVIDENCE_FOUND_BUT_REQUIRE_HUMAN_REVIEW_BEFORE_PERSIST_IMPLEMENTATION

nextStepDecision:
P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-M - Controlled Activity Intake Persist Helper Implementation Draft

Important interpretation
This audit is static and local.
It does not execute SQL.
It does not query Supabase.
It does not create or modify database rows.
A future persist implementation must still use trusted server context and must not trust ownership fields from the client payload.

Provisional future persist mapping to confirm/reuse only after review
- activityEventDraft.title -> confirmed title/name column
- activityEventDraft.inputText -> confirmed raw input / description / text column
- activityEventDraft.source -> confirmed source column or metadata trace
- activityEventDraft.status -> confirmed status column or nearest existing safe status enum/value
- activityEventDraft.durationMinutes -> confirmed duration column if present
- activityEventDraft.startedAt -> confirmed start column if present
- activityEventDraft.endedAt -> confirmed end column if present
- activityEventDraft.occurredAt -> confirmed occurred/event timestamp column if present
- activityEventDraft.timezone -> confirmed timezone column if present
- activityEventDraft.context and metadata -> confirmed metadata/details/json column
- idempotency trace -> confirmed metadata/json column first, unless a later migration adds dedicated idempotency storage
- trusted user/actor/org/space context -> server-owned columns only, never client-provided fields

Hard forbidden future persist behavior
- No AI call.
- No Category Derivation run.
- No categoryCandidates persistence.
- No metricCandidates persistence.
- No stateHookCandidates persistence.
- No Value Object creation.
- No value_object_state_facts writes.
- No activity_state_deltas writes.
- No snapshots writes.
- No runtime route logic inside helper.
- No client-provided ownership fields.

    53: -- 3. category_derivation_runs
    54: -- One versioned semantic interpretation attempt for one activity event.
    55: create table if not exists public.category_derivation_runs (
    56:   id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
    57:   activity_event_id uuid references public.activity_events(id) on delete cascade,
    58:   actor_id uuid null,
    59:   organization_id uuid null,
    60:   input_text text null,
    61:   input_language text null,
    62:   processor_version text not null default 'category_derivation_v1',
    63:   rule_version text null,
    64:   model_name text null,
    65:   prompt_version text null,
    66:   status text not null default 'started',
    67:   confidence numeric null,
    68:   needs_user_confirmation boolean not null default false,
    69:   started_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    70:   finished_at timestamptz null,
    71:   input_json jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
    72:   output_json jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
    73:   error_json jsonb null,
    74:   created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    75:   updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    76:   constraint category_derivation_runs_confidence_range
    77:     check (confidence is null or (confidence >= 0 and confidence <= 1))
    78: );

FILE: docs/sql/P4.10.0-C8-O3_verify_route_derivation_rows.sql
    12: -- commute-to-work
    13: -- walking-to-work
    14: -- duration-minutes
    15: 
    16: with target as (
    17:   select
    18:     '7bf83e7b-02f8-4882-8e7b-419c8843cee2'::uuid as event_id,
    19:     'dd0db584-cad7-4925-9e2a-732a0676e174'::uuid as derivation_run_id,
    20:     array[
    21:       'walking',
    22:       'work',
    23:       'commute-to-work',
    24:       'walking-to-work',
    25:       'duration-minutes'
    26:     ]::text[] as expected_slugs
    27: ),
    28: run_row as (
    29:   select r.*
    30:   from category_derivation_runs r
    31:   join target t
    32:     on r.id = t.derivation_run_id
    33:    and r.activity_event_id = t.event_id
    34: ),
    35: event_row as (
    36:   select e.*
    37:   from activity_events e
    38:   join target t
    39:     on e.id = t.event_id
    40: ),
    41: derivation_rows as (
    42:   select d.*
    43:   from activity_category_derivations d
    44:   join target t
    45:     on d.derivation_run_id = t.derivation_run_id
    46:    and d.activity_event_id = t.event_id
    47: ),
    48: expected_slug_rows as (
    49:   select unnest(t.expected_slugs) as expected_slug
    50:   from target t
    51: ),
    52: slug_check as (
    53:   select
    54:     e.expected_slug,
    55:     exists (
    56:       select 1
    57:       from derivation_rows d
    58:       where d.candidate_slug = e.expected_slug
    59:     ) as exists_ok
    60:   from expected_slug_rows e
    61: ),
    62: summary as (
    63:   select
    64:     (select count(*) from run_row) as run_rows_count,
    65:     (select count(*) from event_row) as event_rows_count,
    66:     (select count(*) from derivation_rows) as derivation_rows_count,
    67:     (select count(*) from slug_check where exists_ok) as expected_slugs_found_count,
    68:     (select count(*) from expected_slug_rows) as expected_slugs_count,
    69:     (select coalesce(bool_and(exists_ok), false) from slug_check) as all_expected_slugs_found,
    70:     (
    71:       select coalesce(bool_and(category_id is null), false)
    72:       from derivation_rows
    73:     ) as all_category_ids_null_expected_for_dry_run,
    74:     (
    75:       select coalesce(bool_and(metadata_json ->> 'resolutionStatus' = 'unresolved'), false)
    76:       from derivation_rows
    77:     ) as all_resolution_status_unresolved,
    78:     (
    79:       select coalesce(bool_and(source = 'rule'), false)
    80:       from derivation_rows
    81:     ) as all_sources_rule
    82: )

FILE: docs/sql/P4.7.8-R-K1_known_template_chain_audit.sql
     1: -- P4.7.8-R-K1
     2: -- Known-template Activity -> Rubricator -> Value Object chain audit
     3: --
     4: -- Purpose:
     5: -- Quickly verify the production chain:
     6: -- activity_event -> approved entity_classification -> VOI/link -> state_delta -> daily_aggregate -> current_snapshot
     7: --
     8: -- Usage:
     9: -- 1) Replace the UUID in target_event with the event id you want to audit.
    10: -- 2) Run this SQL in Supabase SQL Editor.
    11: -- 3) Check summary.flags and returned rows.
    12: --
    13: -- Default event below is the latest verified updated_at proof event from 16.05 documentation.
    14: -- Replace it with a newly created route-level event when testing record/complete/confirm.
    15: 
    16: WITH target_event AS (
    17:   SELECT '9a6321ec-fd38-4294-8dea-273efbc3a057'::uuid AS event_id
    18: ),
    19: 
    20: activity_event_rows AS (
    21:   SELECT ae.*
    22:   FROM activity_events ae
    23:   JOIN target_event t ON ae.id = t.event_id
    24: ),
    25: 
    26: classification_rows AS (
    27:   SELECT ec.*
    28:   FROM entity_classifications ec
    29:   CROSS JOIN target_event t
    30:   WHERE (to_jsonb(ec)->>'entity_id') = t.event_id::text
    31:      OR (to_jsonb(ec)->>'activity_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    32:      OR (to_jsonb(ec)->>'source_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    33: ),
    34: 
    35: voi_link_rows AS (
    36:   SELECT l.*
    37:   FROM activity_event_value_object_instance_links l
    38:   CROSS JOIN target_event t
    39:   WHERE (to_jsonb(l)->>'event_id') = t.event_id::text
    40:      OR (to_jsonb(l)->>'activity_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    41: ),
    42: 
    43: voi_ids_from_links AS (
    44:   SELECT DISTINCT NULLIF(to_jsonb(l)->>'value_object_instance_id', '') AS voi_id
    45:   FROM voi_link_rows l
    46:   WHERE NULLIF(to_jsonb(l)->>'value_object_instance_id', '') IS NOT NULL
    47: ),
    48: 
    49: voi_rows AS (
    50:   SELECT voi.*
    51:   FROM value_object_instances voi
    52:   CROSS JOIN target_event t
    53:   WHERE (to_jsonb(voi)->>'source_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    54:      OR (to_jsonb(voi)->>'activity_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    55:      OR (to_jsonb(voi)->>'event_id') = t.event_id::text
    56:      OR (to_jsonb(voi)->>'id') IN (SELECT voi_id FROM voi_ids_from_links)
    57: ),
    58: 
    59: voi_ids AS (
    60:   SELECT DISTINCT NULLIF(to_jsonb(voi)->>'id', '') AS voi_id
    61:   FROM voi_rows voi
    62:   WHERE NULLIF(to_jsonb(voi)->>'id', '') IS NOT NULL
    63: 
    64:   UNION
    65: 
    66:   SELECT DISTINCT voi_id
    67:   FROM voi_ids_from_links
==========
FILE: docs/sql/P4.7.8-R-L3_lightweight_known_template_chain_audit.sql
     5: -- Fast regression check for the verified chain without returning large row arrays.
     6: --
     7: -- Usage:
     8: -- 1) Replace the UUID in target_event.
     9: -- 2) Run in Supabase SQL Editor.
    10: -- 3) Check flags.completeKnownTemplateChain.
    11: --
    12: -- Verified example events:
    13: -- record route:
    14: --   d42ca357-df9e-4d42-af0d-9c17898c9a05
    15: -- complete route:
    16: --   810fc9fc-1524-4d20-b3ca-68c8301cf5ed
    17: -- confirm route:
    18: --   8a1de3d2-d453-43ad-ae13-1f3c09eb12d5
    19: -- L2.4 runtime record:
    20: --   f27854d9-989d-4618-b2ce-ef3e7676f715
    21: -- L2.4 runtime complete:
    22: --   94bdac48-7ddf-4413-a5de-dfc6721e42ec

    26: ),

    29:   SELECT ae.*
    30:   FROM activity_events ae
    31:   JOIN target_event t ON ae.id = t.event_id
    32: ),
    33: 
    34: classification_rows AS (

    36:   FROM entity_classifications ec
    37:   CROSS JOIN target_event t
    38:   WHERE (to_jsonb(ec)->>'entity_id') = t.event_id::text
    39:      OR (to_jsonb(ec)->>'activity_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    40:      OR (to_jsonb(ec)->>'source_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    41: ),
    42: 
    43: voi_link_rows AS (
    44:   SELECT l.*
    45:   FROM activity_event_value_object_instance_links l
    46:   CROSS JOIN target_event t
    47:   WHERE (to_jsonb(l)->>'event_id') = t.event_id::text
    48:      OR (to_jsonb(l)->>'activity_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    49: ),
    50: 
    51: voi_ids_from_links AS (
    52:   SELECT DISTINCT NULLIF(to_jsonb(l)->>'value_object_instance_id', '') AS voi_id
    53:   FROM voi_link_rows l
    54:   WHERE NULLIF(to_jsonb(l)->>'value_object_instance_id', '') IS NOT NULL
    55: ),
    56: 
    57: voi_rows AS (
    58:   SELECT voi.*
    59:   FROM value_object_instances voi
    60:   CROSS JOIN target_event t
    61:   WHERE (to_jsonb(voi)->>'source_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    62:      OR (to_jsonb(voi)->>'activity_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    63:      OR (to_jsonb(voi)->>'event_id') = t.event_id::text
    64:      OR (to_jsonb(voi)->>'id') IN (SELECT voi_id FROM voi_ids_from_links)
    65: ),
    66: 
    67: voi_ids AS (
    68:   SELECT DISTINCT NULLIF(to_jsonb(voi)->>'id', '') AS voi_id
    69:   FROM voi_rows voi
    70:   WHERE NULLIF(to_jsonb(voi)->>'id', '') IS NOT NULL
    71:

    75:   FROM voi_ids_from_links

    26: --   globalFlags.allRoutesPassed = true

    64: ),
    65: 
    66: voi_link_rows as (
    67:   select
    68:     t.route_name,
    69:     l.*
    70:   from target_events t
    71:   join activity_event_value_object_instance_links l
    72:     on (to_jsonb(l)->>'event_id') = t.event_id::text
    73:     or (to_jsonb(l)->>'activity_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    74: ),
    75: 
    76: voi_ids_from_links as (
    77:   select distinct
    78:     route_name,
    79:     nullif(to_jsonb(voi_link_rows)->>'value_object_instance_id', '') as voi_id
    80:   from voi_link_rows
    81:   where nullif(to_jsonb(voi_link_rows)->>'value_object_instance_id', '') is not null
    82: ),
    83: 
    84: voi_rows as (
    85:   select
    86:     t.route_name,
    87:     voi.*
    88:   from target_events t
    89:   join value_object_instances voi
    90:     on (to_jsonb(voi)->>'source_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    91:     or (to_jsonb(voi)->>'activity_event_id') = t.event_id::text
    92:     or (to_jsonb(voi)->>'event_id') = t.event_id::text
    93:     or (to_jsonb(voi)->>'id') in (
    94:       select voi_id
    95:       from voi_ids_from_links ids
    96:       where ids.route_name = t.route_name

FILE: docs/sql/P4.7.9-R-A3_known_template_metadata_normalization.sql
     1: -- P4.7.9-R-A3
     2: -- Known-template metadata normalization and post-normalization audit.
     3: --
     4: -- Date: 2026-05-16
     5: -- Scope:
     6: --   1) Normalize activity_templates.default_metadata_json for two known templates.
     7: --   2) Keep changes limited to default_metadata_json.
     8: --   3) Verify metadata can work as transitional DB-backed registry v0.1.
     9: --
    10: -- Templates:
    11: --   - german-marketing-handwriting-practice
    12: --   - knee-training-health-practice
    13: --
    14: -- Safety:
    15: --   - Updates ONLY public.activity_templates.default_metadata_json.
    16: --   - Does NOT touch activity_events.
    17: --   - Does NOT touch entity_classifications.
    18: --   - Does NOT touch value_objects.
    19: --   - Does NOT touch impact_rules.
    20: --   - Idempotent: repeated run should update 0 rows after first successful run.
    21: --
    22: -- Verified result from live DB:
    23: --   bothTemplatesHaveKnownTemplateRegistry = true
    24: --   bothTemplatesHaveRubricatorCandidate = true
    25: --   bothTemplatesHaveValueObjectTitle = true
    26: --   bothTemplatesHaveValueObjectMapping = true
    27: --   a3cReadyToDocumentAndCommit = true
    28: 
    29: -- ============================================================
    30: -- A3b: idempotent metadata normalization
    31: -- ============================================================
    32: 
    33: drop table if exists pg_temp.p479_a3b_before;
    34: drop table if exists pg_temp.p479_a3b_desired;
    35: drop table if exists pg_temp.p479_a3b_updated;
    36: 
    37: create temp table pg_temp.p479_a3b_before as
    38: select
    39:   t.id,
    40:   t.slug,
    41:   t.default_metadata_json as before_metadata
    42: from public.activity_templates t
    43: where t.slug in (
    44:   'german-marketing-handwriting-practice',
    45:   'knee-training-health-practice'
    46: );
    47: 
    48: create temp table pg_temp.p479_a3b_desired as
    49: select
    50:   t.id,
    51:   t.slug,
    52:   case
    53:     when t.slug = 'german-marketing-handwriting-practice' then
    54:       coalesce(t.default_metadata_json, '{}'::jsonb)
    55:       || jsonb_build_object(
    56:         'knownTemplateRegistry', jsonb_build_object(
    57:           'enabled', true,
    58:           'ruleKey', 'german_marketing_handwriting_practice_to_business_german',
    59:           'templateSlug', 'german-marketing-handwriting-practice',
    60:           'sourceType', 'system_seed',
    61:           'classificationRole', 'primary',

FILE: docs/sql/P4.9.5-A1_create_value_object_cloud_profiles_v1.sql
     1: /*
     2: P4.9.5-A1
     3: Create reusable object-cloud read interface.
     4: 
     5: Creates:
     6: - public.value_object_cloud_profiles_v1
     7: 
     8: Purpose:
     9: - reusable read model over v4.2 runtime foundation;
    10: - one row per user + Value Object + contextual category link;
    11: - combines usage, category, latest exposure, snapshots and daily aggregates.
    12: 
    13: Tables used:
    14: - value_object_category_links
    15: - contextual_categories
    16: - value_object_usage_aggregates
    17: - activity_event_value_object_links
    18: - activity_events
    19: - activity_templates
    20: - value_object_state_snapshots
    21: - value_object_daily_aggregates
    22: 
    23: Important:
    24: - This is a read interface.
    25: - It does not change runtime writer logic.
    26: - It does not replace old VOI pipeline.
    27: */
    28:

    44:     cc.is_active AS contextual_category_is_active
    45:   FROM public.value_object_category_links cl
    46:   LEFT JOIN public.contextual_categories cc
    47:     ON cl.category_table = 'contextual_categories'
    48:    AND cc.id = cl.category_id
    49: ),
    50:

    60:     ua.last_event_id,
    61:     ua.source AS usage_source,
    62:     ua.metadata_json AS usage_metadata_json,
    63:     ua.created_at AS usage_created_at,

     4: -- activity_links, time_blocks, value_objects, organizations, offers, certificates or points.
     5: --
     6: -- Main rule:

FILE: supabase/migrations/014_activity_events_v2_template_link.sql
     1: -- 014_activity_events_v2_template_link.sql
     2: -- Activity Recording Layer v2 compatibility patch.
     3: --
     4: -- Purpose:
     5: -- activity_events.template_id from migration 012 points to legacy activity_code_templates.
     6: -- New v2 template-first model uses activity_templates.
     7: -- Therefore we add activity_events.activity_template_id instead of overloading template_id.
     8: --
     9: -- This migration is additive and does not modify legacy activities, bookings,
    10: -- activity_participants, activity_links, time_blocks, value_objects or commercial core.
    11: 
    12: create extension if not exists "pgcrypto";
    13: 
    14: alter table public.activity_events
    15: add column if not exists activity_template_id uuid
    16: references public.activity_templates(id)
    17: on delete set null;
    18: 
    19: create index if not exists idx_activity_events_activity_template_id
    20: on public.activity_events(activity_template_id);
    21: 
    22: drop trigger if exists trg_activity_events_updated_at on public.activity_events;
    23: 
    24: create trigger trg_activity_events_updated_at
    25: before update on public.activity_events
    26: for each row
    27: execute function public.set_activity_recording_updated_at();
    28: 
    29: alter table public.activity_events
    30: drop constraint if exists activity_events_source_check;
    31: 
    32: alter table public.activity_events
    33: add constraint activity_events_source_check
    34: check (
    35:   source in (
    36:     -- legacy/source values from 012
    37:     'manual',
    38:     'chat_ai',
    39:     'calendar',
    40:     'booking',
    41:     'rule',
    42:     'import',
    43:     'system',
    44: 
    45:     -- v2 source_type values
    46:     'manual_form',

     4: -- Purpose:
     5: -- activity_events.template_id from migration 012 points to legacy activity_code_templates.
     6: -- New v2 template-first model uses activity_templates.
     7: -- Therefore we add activity_events.activity_template_id instead of overloading template_id.
     8: --
     9: -- This migration is additive and does not modify legacy activities, bookings,
    10: -- activity_participants, activity_links, time_blocks, value_objects or commercial core.
    11: 
    12: create extension if not exists "pgcrypto";
    13: 
    14: alter table public.activity_events
    15: add column if not exists activity_template_id uuid
    16: references public.activity_templates(id)
    17: on delete set null;
    18: 
    19: create index if not exists idx_activity_events_activity_template_id
    20: on public.activity_events(activity_template_id);
    21: 
    22: drop trigger if exists trg_activity_events_updated_at on public.activity_events;
    23: 
    24: create trigger trg_activity_events_updated_at
    25: before update on public.activity_events
    26: for each row
    27: execute function public.set_activity_recording_updated_at();
    28: 
    29: alter table public.activity_events
    30: drop constraint if exists activity_events_source_check;
    31: 
    32: alter table public.activity_events
    33: add constraint activity_events_source_check
    34: check (
    35:   source in (
    36:     -- legacy/source values from 012
    37:     'manual',
    38:     'chat_ai',
    39:     'calendar',
    40:     'booking',
    41:     'rule',
    42:     'import',
    43:     'system',
    44: 
    45:     -- v2 source_type values
    46:     'manual_form',
    47:     'manual_chat',
    48:     'voice_input',
    49:     'app_action',
    50:     'system_event',
    51:     'api_webhook',
    52:     'nfc_sensor',
    53:     'wearable_import',
    54:     'calendar_import',
    55:     'ai_suggested',
    56:     'legacy_code'
    57:   )
    58: );
    59:
